# T-0018 cases (register promotion of globals and related register-order gaps)

Data file of [[tickets/T-1321-register-promotion-build-step]]. One row per function skipped (left `INCLUDE_ASM` or `NON_MATCHING`) because of the T-0018 gap ([[matching-notes]], section "Register promotion of globals"). `tools/queue.py --calibrate` reads this table to measure the blocker detector, so keep the format.

Rules for batch agents (decompile-workflow step 7): append one row per skipped case, never edit or delete a row.
- `file`: main address stem (`8005A0B0`) or overlay name (`TEL`), as in the `file` column of `tools/queue.py`.
- `function`: name in backticks.
- `category`: `promo` if the original keeps one global in one register across several blocks (loads it again after calls or branches, same register) and IDO uses other registers; `reverse` if the original loads the global again into fresh temporaries where IDO keeps it in a register (the opposite gap); `regorder` for any other T-0018 register-choice difference (temporary order, `$v0`/`$v1` of a value that is not a global reload).
- `symptom`: one short line: what the original has and what IDO emits.

Update (T-10400): `tools/regdiff.py` explains a row's register difference from the allocator trace ([[decompile-workflow]]). Rows now matched in plain C with it: EVENT `func_80119518` (local copy of the selector), DATE `func_80136F2C` (no temporary for the copied value). Run it before recording a new row.

Update (T-1321): `tools/cvt_pass.py` now reproduces most `promo` rows (switch or compare chain on an unsigned global, reloads after calls); see [[matching-notes]], "Unsigned-load conversion pass (T-1321)". The rows stay as the test set; T-3000 retries them.

Update (T-5010): `tools/cvt_pass.py` is in the build, with an entry rule (only globals touched before the first call or branch) and a compare rule; unit-private selectors that the original keeps in `$v0` are written as a switch on a `FAKE` local copy. Batch guidance and numbers: [[matching-notes]], "Selector register rule (T-5010)". Keep recording rows as before: they stay the test set.

Update (T-7000): the build runs IDO in K&R mode with the widening of narrow globals ([[matching-notes]], "K&R promotion rules (T-7000)"). Rows now matched with plain C: TACO `func_8014EDCC`, EVENT `func_8011A2C4`, `func_8011A4A4`, `func_80119600`, `func_80102C0C`, DATE `func_8013BDB4`, TT `func_8013C764`, main `func_8007B5EC`, `SD_DetectCDPeak`. The `li at,k` per compare and the K&R parameter copies are no longer T-0018 shapes; U1 selectors after a call (main `func_800626B0`, `func_8005AE60`) still are.

Update (T-9000): re-tested under K&R with the wave agents' drafts ([[matching-notes]], "T-0018 rows under K&R: re-test, clusters, pass verdict (T-9000)"). Rows now matched in plain C: GYOZI `func_8013B6CC`, `func_8013B740`, `func_8013CAA4`, `func_8013BC40`, `func_8013BCB4`, `func_8013B644`, SHOUGATU `func_80138E60`, `func_80138ED4`, `func_80139E54` (bit-field flag store); SHUGAKU `func_80134024`, `func_8013A980`, GEKO `func_8013E56C`, `func_80140EBC`, SHOUGATU `func_80138158`, DATE `func_80148924`, TACO `func_8014E1A0`, OLH `func_80136210` (`s32` without a return value); EVENT `func_8010AC40` (chain assignment); RPG_BAT `func_80144640`, DATE `func_80145DF0` (draft unchanged). RPG_BAT `func_80142544` matches as `s32` but waits for the `D_8012121C` declaration. Before recording a new row, try `s32` without a return value and a bit-field store ([[decompile-workflow]]).

Update (T-16230): rows for DATE `func_801511B4`, `func_80150460` and `func_80150C78` (T-4010, T-9110, T-9320, T-9580, T-9780) are now matched in plain C, with `func_80150ED4`, by naming the real aggregates (`D_801217D0[57]`, display records 96 and 98 of `D_8011ECD0`), a chain assignment through record elements, and a `GameState *g` pointer ([[matching-notes]], "T-16230").

Update (T-19015 closer): TACO `func_801471D0` (rows T-11300, T-16561, T-19015: the `move s4,s3; lw s6,4(s4); addiu s4,s4,8` copy) is matched in plain C. Both passes walk one pointer `dop` from the parameter: `dop = arg0; dop++; f(dop); dop += 2;` for loop 1 and the same restart `dop = arg0; dop++; f(dop); dop++; n = *dop; dop++;` for loop 2. uopt keeps the second walk as `dop = T + 4` and in-place steps (as1 then makes the move), but only with a pointer parameter: with `s32 arg0` and a cast it keeps the loop-1 copy too. The callers keep the `s32` view through `TACO_OVERRIDE_func_801471D0` (a `u32 *` prototype changes the caller in `80148FE0.c`).

Update (T-19001): TAIIKU `func_80140A90` stays guarded (33 instructions). The block copy's temporary count was measured as a two-state rule (one skip after a loop or pointer store, two after a global/stack store, a call or at entry) and no code-neutral C changes it; the original also shows the opposite state elsewhere (SHUGAKU `func_801357AC`, MASTER, ENDING), so this looks like a ugen difference ([[matching-notes]], "T-19001").

Update (T-19012): OMIMAI `func_80132670` (rows `promo` T-9120 and `scalar-global` T-9320) is now matched in plain C with one `FAKE`: the first `unk_75D` read as `*(u8 *)&D_800E6280.unk_75D` into a `u32 x`, `u8 a = x;`, and the read placed before the `D_800CA14C = 0;` store. The rows stay as history. `func_8013388C` is a new row at the end.

Update (T-16552 closer): rows for TAIIKU `func_80138DF8` and `func_80138AA8` (T-11640, T-16552) are now matched. `func_80138DF8`: `i = 0` between the `cd_read_sync_status()` call and its test, and a `goto` into the `for` loop so it has no entry test (FAKE). `func_80138AA8`: `func_80085E30` declared as an implicit int function in the file (`MAIN_API_OVERRIDE_func_80085E30`), and a real local `p` for the case 2 buffer address instead of the frame pad. See [[matching-notes]], "T-16552".
Update (T-16553): TAIIKU `func_80146A60` (rows T-4100 and w7-6: flag word in `$v0`, masks in `$v1`) is matched in plain C by copying the flag word into a local before the mask tests; `func_80147928` (same flag word) matches too ([[matching-notes]], "T-16553").

Update (T-16553 closer pass): TAIIKU `func_80145640` (counter spill slot one word too high) is matched in plain C: the call result goes into a declared local `slot`, declared before the counter, instead of nesting the call in the chain assignment. `func_8014607C` stays guarded; the closer pass found that IDO propagates both local copies (`var = D_800E638E`, `mask = D_800E6375 & 0xF`) into their uses after the `||`, and the original does not ([[matching-notes]], T-16553).
Update (T-16562, closer pass): TACO `func_80154230`, `func_801546FC`, `func_8015443C` (rows T-9330, T-10310, T-10850 and the T-16562 rows) and `func_801345D4` are now matched in plain C. The entry copy is as1's copy propagation, and a two-step walk of the header (`arg0++; ... arg0 += 8;`) keeps `move s0,a0`; the `func_801345D4` address comes back when the flag test reads the word through the same base symbol ([[matching-notes]], "T-16562", "Closer pass"). Same as1 shape, not yet retried: TACO `func_801471D0` (T-11300).
Update (T-16551, closer pass): rows for TAIIKU `func_801346FC` (T-10890, T-12020, T-16551) and `func_80136BB4` (T-12020, T-16551) are now matched in plain C: a `void` prototype for the callee `func_801365C8` (its `s32` declaration kept `$v0` busy in the call's block), and a `u16` local copy of the f0C counter for its tests (the field is then promoted into the join block and its store-back follows the f03 store-back) ([[matching-notes]], "T-16551").
Update (T-16561): TACO `func_80147928` (row below, 4 lines left at the time) is matched in plain C with an empty `do { } while (0);` after `base = arg2 + 4;` and the declaration order `p, pos, slot, base` (FAKE, found by `tools/permute.py` in 150 s); `func_80159878`, `func_8013788C`, `func_8013760C`, `func_80147C98`, `func_80146F74`, `func_80147674` and `func_80147488` are matched as well ([[matching-notes]], T-16561).

Update (T-19004): TAIIKU `func_80137228` (rows T-9140, T-10520, T-10620, T-10720: `D_8014929A` hoisted, `D_80120A72` reloaded) is matched in plain C. `D_80120A72` and `D_80120AA8` are fields of display records 111 and 112, so the loop's record stores force the reload while `D_80149298.halves.high` is hoisted; no local `x` or `d` ([[matching-notes]], T-19004).

Update (T-19005): rows for OLH `func_80132114` (T-9350, T-9780) and `func_80135B8C` (T-9780) are matched in plain C (one FAKE `pad` and one FAKE `col` local). `func_80135B8C` is a direct port of its three 1.000-ratio siblings (`tools/siblings.py`); `func_80132114` needs the element chain through a declared local, see [[register-cheatsheet]] and [[matching-notes]], "T-19005".
Update (T-19008): TAIIKU `func_8013B200` (rows T-11640, T-16552, `promo`) is matched in FAKE plain C: no local for the shifted position, `D_801499A4` before `D_801499A6`, flag through `((u16 *)D_801499B0)[1]` ([[matching-notes]], "T-19008"). `func_8013BCEC` is a guarded draft of 18 lines; `func_8013B608` is a guarded draft of 18 lines (FAKE `s16 px`). Update (T-19007): the TAIIKU `801452D0` row for `func_80146D18` (T-16553, 31 lines) is now matched in plain C. Both gaps came from the hand-shaped loops of the draft: `for (k = 0; &tbl.v[k] < &tbl.v[4]; k++)` puts the record store after the pointer advance, and `k = 0; while (D_8014A3D8[k] != 0) { k++; } D_8014A3D8[k] = 1; for (i = k + 1; i < 4; i++) D_8014A3D8[i] = func_80146FA0();` replaces the `if`/`do`/pointer-walk form (the search index named `k`, the variable of the loops before it, lands in `$a0`). Rows for the other three functions of the file are in the last lines of this table.
Update (T-19016): VALEN `func_80133670` (rows T-4050, T-8010, T-9020, T-9760, T-10030, T-10520, T-10710: call-argument base register `lui v0` against `lui a0`) and `func_80133728` (same symptom twice) are now matched in plain C by reading the selected girl through a local `GameState *g = &D_800E6280;` (`g->unk_F5F` at every use); no member, cast, byte-index or callee-declaration spelling did it. The same idea is untried for the other rows with this symptom (SHUGAKU `func_80139E8C`, DATE2 `func_80136794`, EVENT `func_8010B614`, main `func_80042960`; not SHOUGATU `func_801347E0`, whose original has `lui a0`). See [[matching-notes]], "T-19016".

## T-9410 resolutions (2026-10-11)

Historical rows above remain unchanged. Resolution records:
- **Resolved: 8005A0B0 / `func_8005AE60`**, the batch-D `promo` row: a `u8 state` local loaded after the call reproduces the selector in `$v1`; plain C.
- **Resolved: ENDING/80137340 / `func_80138278`**, the w7-4 remainder row: a marked FAKE dead test before `% 240` gives `$v1` and the original temporary numbering.
- The same remainder family also gains `func_80138FC8`, `func_801391E4` and `func_8013855C`, discussed in the wave-7 notes rather than separate rows here. The last also needs a marked FAKE `s16 one` compare constant.

Evidence and unresolved experiments: [[register-allocation-wave7]]. Idioms: [[decompile-workflow]].
